Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The enumerative variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 391 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 334 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the enumerative decomposition. By minimality, the element a^391 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 334-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
